Our key finding is that rule-based skills alone do not constitute a strong strategy, and LLMs alone cannot play well, but their combination yields an agent that requires neither training nor solver access yet competes with systems built on millions of core-hours of computation. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of an LLM achieving competitive performance in a complex imperfect-information game without game-specific training or solver queries. Code is available at https://github.com/lbn187/PokerSkill.

</details>


### [309] [VLA-Trace: Diagnosing Vision-Language-Action Models through Representation and Behavior Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30117)
*Haoyuan Shi,Xiancong Ren,Yingji Zhang,Qinfan Zhang,Jiayu Hu,Haozhe Shan,Han Dong,Jinpeng Lu,Yinda Chen,Yi Zhang,Yong Dai,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VLA-Trace诊断框架，通过统一的证据链分析视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型如何将多模态知识转化为具身控制，揭示了模型在微调动态、多模态路由策略和语义跟随能力方面的关键特性与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型如何将多模态知识转化为具身控制机制仍是一个开放性挑战，缺乏系统性的诊断方法来分析其内部工作原理。

Method: 提出VLA-Trace渐进式诊断框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 结合跨模态和检查点漂移的中心核对齐（CKA）追踪表征演化；(2) 注意力淘汰干预识别模态特定控制路径；(3) 展开级别的行为探针检查接地、捷径依赖和语义跟随能力。

Result: 在π₀.₅和OpenVLA模型上的实验揭示了三个关键发现：(1) 两个模型在VLA微调过程中展现出不同的模态特定适应动态；(2) 它们在动作解码时依赖不同的多模态路由策略和层级依赖关系；(3) VLA策略虽然擅长视觉接地的轨迹生成，但在细粒度语义跟随方面仍有局限。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明未来需要在三个方面进行改进：表征保持的适应方法、因果VLA电路的构建以及组合语义控制机制的发展。

Abstract: Understanding how Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models transform multimodal knowledge into embodied control remains an open challenge. We present VLA-Trace, a progressive diagnostic framework that analyzes VLA models through a unified evidence chain from representation dynamics to causal control attribution and behavioral manifestation. It specifically combines cross-modal and checkpoint-drift centered kernel alignment (CKA) to trace representation evolution, attention knockout interventions to identify modality-specific control pathways, and rollout-level behavioral probes to examine grounding, shortcut dependence, and semantic following. Experiments on $π_{0.5}$ and OpenVLA reveal three key findings. First, the two models exhibit distinct modality-specific adaptation dynamics during VLA finetuning. Second, they rely on different multimodal routing strategies and layer-wise dependencies during action decoding. Third, although VLA policies excel at visually grounded trajectory generation, they remain limited in fine-grained semantic following. These findings highlight future directions for representation-preserving adaptation, causal VLA circuits, and compositional semantic control.

</details>


### [310] [Enhancing Multi-Agent Communication through Attention Steering with Context Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30136)
*Hongxiang Zhang,Yuan Tian,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Agent-Radar，一种无需训练的上下文管理方法，通过时空衰减机制动态引导多智能体系统关注相关上下文，在五个基准测试上超越现有方法，性能提升达7.64个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体系统在协作推理复杂任务时表现出色，但交互过程中会迅速积累极长的对话历史。随着对话变长，相关信息被无关上下文稀释，导致系统性能下降。

Method: 提出Agent-Radar，一种无需训练的上下文管理方法。该方法通过新颖的时间和空间衰减机制，动态引导每个智能体的注意力集中于相关上下文，从而有效管理长对话历史。

Result: 在五个不同基准测试中，Agent-Radar均超越当前最先进方法，绝对性能提升最高达7.64个百分点。分析表明，随着智能体数量和交互轮数的增加，Agent-Radar依然保持有效性和鲁棒性。消融实验验证了核心组件对性能的关键作用及在不同设置下的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Agent-Radar为LLM多智能体系统提供了一种高效、鲁棒的上下文管理解决方案，能够有效缓解长对话历史导致的信息稀释问题，显著提升系统性能，且具有良好的可扩展性和泛化性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems have demonstrated remarkable performance on complex tasks through collaborative reasoning. However, these systems tend to rapidly accumulate extremely long conversation histories during interaction. As conversations lengthen, relevant information is increasingly diluted by irrelevant context, leading to degraded performance. In this work, we present Agent-Radar, a training-free context management method that dynamically steers each agent's attention toward relevant context with a novel temporal and spatial decay mechanism. Our experiments demonstrate that Agent-Radar outperforms state-of-the-art methods across five different benchmarks, yielding gains of up to 7.64 absolute points. Furthermore, our analysis shows that Agent-Radar remains effective and robust as the number of agents and interaction rounds increases. Finally, the ablation study shows that core components in Agent-Radar are crucial to performance and generalizable in different settings.

</details>


### [311] [Anchorless Diversification for Parallel LLM Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30150)
*Fares Nabil Ibrahim,Nafis Saami Azad,Raiyan Abdul Baten*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM生成创意候选池时的推理时控制策略，发现语义方向分层方法在多样性-质量-计算成本权衡上表现最优，优于基于锚点的生成方法，为开放式LLM创意任务建立了实用的无锚基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM广泛用于创意任务的候选想法池生成，并行推理在扩大候选池的同时需要保持质量和成本效率。现有方法多依赖观察到的种子想法作为锚点，但锚点方法存在局限性。本文旨在探索无锚点方法是否能在候选池多样化中与锚点方法相媲美。

Method: 在三个创意任务族上，比较了独立生成和语义方向分层两种无锚方法，与自锚点、同伴锚点、代表性锚点三类锚点基线方法进行对比。实验在两种不同的发散性指令设置下进行：中性指令和群体参照发散指令，并进行了全流程token成本核算。

Result: 群体参照发散是一个强力的低成本基线，在增加语义多样性的同时保持质量指标。语义方向分层方法表现更强：仅通过一次规划调用即可组织跨广泛语义方向的生成，达到最佳的多样性-质量-计算成本前沿。锚点重生成方法在最终池多样性上有优势，但在完整token成本核算下优势缩小。

Conclusion: 研究建立了开放式LLM创意生成的实用无锚基线方法，证明了语义方向分层方法在实际应用中的有效性，为LLM创意任务提供了成本高效的解决方案。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used to generate candidate-idea pools for creative tasks where broad exploration is valuable. Parallel inference can be attractive in this setting when it broadens the pool while retaining quality and cost efficiency. We study inference-time controls for candidate-pool diversification, asking whether anchorless methods can rival methods that depend on observed seed ideas. Across three creative task families, we compare independent generation and semantic direction stratification with self-, peer-, and representative-anchor baselines, under neutral and population-referential divergent instructions. Population-referential divergence is a strong low-cost baseline, increasing semantic diversity while preserving quality proxies. Semantic direction stratification is stronger: a single planning call organizes generations across broad semantic directions, yielding the best diversity--quality--compute frontier. Anchored regeneration can be strong in final-pool diversity, but its advantage shrinks under full-pipeline token accounting. These results establish practical anchorless baselines for open-ended LLM ideation.

</details>


### [312] [Temporal Stability and Few-Shot Prompting in Math Task Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30151)
*Danielle S. Fox,Brenda L. Robles,Elizabeth DiPietro Brovey,Christian D. Schunn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了AI工具在数学任务认知需求分类任务中的稳定性，发现模型版本更新效果不一，但少样本提示工程能显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI工具在教育领域的广泛应用，研究者需要了解这些工具在特定教育任务上的性能稳定性，以及模型更新和提示工程技术的实际影响。

Method: 采用纵向研究设计，测试通用AI工具（Gemini）和教育专用AI工具（Coteach）使用任务分析指南（TAG）对数学任务进行认知需求分类的能力。研究包括基线测试、模型版本更新后重测，以及使用少样本提示（每个认知需求类别两个示例任务）后的测试。

Result: 模型版本更新产生混合效果：Gemini准确率稳定在58%，而Coteach准确率从75%降至50%。少样本提示改善了两个模型的性能：Gemini提升至67%，Coteach恢复至75%准确率。

Conclusion: 提示工程技术比被动的模型改进能产生更大、更可靠的效果，版本更新并不总能改善专业教育任务的性能。这对教育工作者和研究者如何选择、评估和实施AI工具具有重要启示。

Abstract: As AI tools become increasingly integrated into educational contexts, questions arise about both their stability over time and their responsiveness to prompt engineering techniques. This longitudinal study focused on different AI tools' ability to use the Task Analysis Guide (TAG; Stein \& Smith, 1998) to classify the cognitive demand of mathematics tasks. In particular, it examined whether this classification ability changed with (1) model version updates over time and (2) few-shot prompting using exemplar tasks. We tested a general-purpose AI tool (Gemini) and an education-specific AI tool (Coteach). The specific tools were selected because of their relatively high performance on relevant published benchmarks and prior task-specific tests. Models were tested at baseline, retested with model version updates, and then tested again using few-shot prompting (two exemplar tasks for each cognitive demand category). Results revealed that newer model versions alone produced mixed effects: Gemini's accuracy remained stable at 58\%, while Coteach's accuracy decreased from 75\% to 50\%. However, few-shot prompting improved both models' performance: Gemini increased to 67\% and Coteach recovered to 75\% accuracy. These findings demonstrate that prompt engineering techniques can have larger and more reliable effects than passive model improvements, and that version updates may not always improve performance on specialized educational tasks. The study has important implications for how educators and researchers should approach AI tool selection, evaluation, and implementation in educational contexts.

</details>


### [313] [Meta-Cognitive Memory Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30159)
*Ziyan Liu,Zhezheng Hao,Yeqiu Chen,Hong Wang,Jingren Hou,Ruiyi Ding,Yongkang Yang,Wence Ji,Wei Xia,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MMPO方法，通过引入信念熵作为自监督代理，惩罚导致高认知不确定性的记忆摘要，从而优化记忆增强LLM代理的长期任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强LLM代理采用基于结果的强化学习训练记忆策略，无法定位中间记忆质量下降的具体位置。随着交互进行，模糊的递归摘要会逐渐丢弃任务相关信息并引入语义噪声，加剧信念偏差，模糊智能体对潜在任务状态的估计，最终导致长期推理失败。

Method: 引入信念熵（Belief Entropy）作为自监督代理，衡量模型在给定当前记忆情况下对潜在任务状态的不确定性；提出元认知记忆策略优化（MMPO），通过显式惩罚诱导高认知不确定性的摘要，提供细粒度、记忆特定的监督信号，而非仅依赖稀疏的基于结果的信号。

Result: MMPO在多种长期任务上持续优于现有方法；在扩展至1.75M token上下文时仍保持97.1%的性能表现。

Conclusion: 记忆优化不应仅关注轨迹级别的成功，而应关注中间摘要所诱导的信念清晰度；通过信念熵引导的细粒度监督能够有效提升记忆增强LLM代理在长期任务中的表现。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents tackle complex long-horizon tasks by recursively summarizing interaction trajectories into compact memory. However, existing approaches typically train these memory policies using outcome-based reinforcement learning, failing to localize where intermediate memory quality degrades. As interactions unfold, ambiguous recursive summaries progressively discard task-relevant information and introduce semantic noise. This exacerbates belief deviation, obscuring the agent's estimate of the latent task state and ultimately derailing long-horizon reasoning. We therefore argue that memory optimization should focus not merely on trajectory-level success, but on the clarity of the belief induced by intermediate summaries. To this end, we introduce Belief Entropy, a self-supervised proxy that probes how uncertain the model remains about the latent task state given its current memory. Based on this proxy, we propose Metacognitive Memory Policy Optimization (MMPO). Instead of relying only on sparse outcome-based signals, MMPO provides fine-grained, memory-specific supervision via explicitly penalizing summaries that induce high epistemic uncertainty. Experiments show that MMPO consistently outperforms existing methods on diverse long-horizon tasks, maintaining 97.1% performance even when scaled to 1.75M-token contexts.

</details>


### [314] [BioRefusalAudit: Auditing Biosecurity Refusal Depth Using General and Domain-Fine-Tuned Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30162)
*Caleb DeLeeuw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究发现语言模型在生物安全拒绝机制上存在严重缺陷：模型无法可靠区分良性与危险内容，拒绝行为受提示词格式、输出长度限制影响极大，且过度拒绝合法科学内容，表明拒绝逻辑可能基于合法性而非实际危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型生物安全评估主要关注模型是否产生危险输出，但缺乏对模型拒绝行为本身质量的评估。作者关注一个补充性问题：当模型拒绝时，这种拒绝是否结构合理且稳健，还是会因提示词框架、格式或输出长度等轻微变化而消失？

Method: 研究团队在五种架构的模型上测试了75个提示词，考察提示词格式（如聊天模板）、输出长度限制（80 token上限）等因素对拒绝行为的影响。同时，引入了"分歧分数D"，比较模型的表面响应标签与内部稀疏自编码器(SAE)特征激活，用于测量内部状态。研究还测试了一批表I管制但生物学上无毒的化合物（如裸盖菇素培育），以探究过度拒绝的驱动因素。

Result: 所有模型均无法清晰区分良性与危险内容：(1)Gemma 2 2B-IT在75个提示词中从未真正拒绝；(2)Gemma 4 E2B-IT使用聊天模板时拒绝65/75，无模板时0/75拒绝，两者在80 token限制下均降至0%；(3)Qwen 2.5 1.5B和Phi-3-mini过度拒绝，将83-87%的良性生物学内容标记为危险；(4)Llama 3.2 1B显示出唯一有意义的梯度差(61分)。部分模型对合法化合物的拒绝率超过真正危险的生物学内容。在Gemma 4上，顺从与拒绝响应之间存在0.647的分值差距且无重叠(n=75)。

Conclusion: 这项在黑客马拉松期间完成的研究提供了初步证据，表明激活层面的审计可能揭示行为评估无法发现的失败模式。语言模型的生物安全拒绝机制存在显著的结构性缺陷，各架构间变异性大，拒绝行为更多追踪合法性与文化显著性而非实际的CBRN危害。需要进一步研究以确认这些发现。

Abstract: Biosecurity evaluations of language models typically ask whether models produce hazardous output. This paper asks a complementary question: when a model refuses, is that refusal structurally sound, or does it disappear under modest changes to prompt framing, formatting, or output length? Across five architectures, no model cleanly discriminated benign from hazard. Gemma 2 2B-IT never genuinely refused across 75 prompts, hedging on every hazard-adjacent query. Gemma 4 E2B-IT refused 65/75 prompts with chat-template formatting and 0/75 without it. Both Gemma models collapsed to 0% under an 80-token cap. Qwen 2.5 1.5B and Phi-3-mini over-refused, flagging 83-87% of benign biology as hazardous. Llama 3.2 1B showed the only meaningful tier gradient (61-point spread). To probe what drives such over-refusal, we tested a panel of Schedule I but biologically non-toxic compounds (notably psilocybin cultivation, with FDA Breakthrough Therapy status). Some models refused these at rates exceeding genuinely hazardous biology, suggesting refusal tracks legality and cultural salience over CBRN hazard. To measure the internal side, we introduce a divergence score D comparing a model's surface response label to its internal sparse autoencoder (SAE) feature activations. Full D was computed on Gemma 2 2B-IT (Gemma Scope 1) and Gemma 4 E2B-IT (author-trained bio SAE). Two fine-tuned Gemma 2 domain SAEs were released. On Gemma 4, comply and refuse responses separated by a 0.647-point gap with zero overlap (n=75), though this is preliminary, with a narrow catalog, within-sample calibration, and Gemma-family-only SAE coverage. Built over one hackathon weekend on consumer hardware (GTX 1650 Ti Max-Q, plus Colab T4 for SAE training), this preliminary evidence suggests activation-level auditing may surface failure modes invisible to behavioral evaluation, with substantial variation across architectures.

</details>


### [315] [Modularizing Educational LLM-Agency for Fostering Responsible Learning Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30187)
*Julius Gabelmann,Felix Jahn,Kevin Baum,Sophie van Rossum,Emely Wuenscher,Timo P. Gros,Verena Wolf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化的智能代理AI聊天机器人架构，用于辅助学生解题，旨在实现更负责任的教育AI应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLM）在教育领域广泛应用，但其本身并不倾向于遵循教育学概念，可能对学习过程产生负面影响，如削弱迁移能力、批判性思维或创造力。现有的一体化解决方案存在结构性缺陷，无法有效支持负责任的教育AI使用。

Method: 基于对负责任LLM教育系统需求的识别，提出模块化的智能代理架构。针对解题过程的不同阶段设计专门模块，融入针对性的教学指导，以更可控、透明和可监督的方式引导学生学习。

Result: 设计了一种模块化的AI聊天机器人架构，能够将教学建议有效整合到解题过程的不同阶段，实现了更可控、透明和可监督的教育AI系统。

Conclusion: 模块化的智能代理架构相比单一的一体化解决方案，能够更好地支持负责任的AI教育应用，为教育领域AI系统的设计提供了新的思路和框架。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of AI chatbots in education will drastically change learning, making responsible deployment a critical concern. While large language models (LLMs) might have access to sources discussing insights from educational sciences, they are not particularly inclined to adhere to pedagogical concepts, risking negative effects on the learning process, such as a loss of transfer capabilities, critical thinking, or creativity. In this paper, we introduce an agentic AI chatbot architecture assisting students with exercise solving, specifically designed to contribute to more responsible AI use in education. We base our conceptual development on the identification of several desiderata for responsible LLM-based educational systems, argue for the structural shortcomings inherent in monolithic, out-of-the-box solutions, and instead suggest modularizing the agentic architecture. We propose specific modules for different stages of exercise solving, enabling incorporation of targeted pedagogical advice, guiding students through the learning process in a more controllable, transparent, and overseeable manner.

</details>


### [316] [Double-Edged Sword or Sharp Tool? Designing and Evaluating Triadic LLM-Teacher Collaboration for K-12 Writing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30200)
*Canran Wang,Yuwen Yang,Zhen Wang,Ming Ma,Ding Yu,Chentai Wang,Keman Huang,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个支持K-12写作学习的LLM-教师-学生三方协作系统，通过大规模实证研究验证了其有效性，发现LLM作为生成引擎减轻教师负担、教师作为教学把关者保证反馈质量的分工模式效果显著，但存在语言过度扩展的边际效用递减问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）在教育领域的整合是一把双刃剑，需要建立LLMs、教师和学生之间的有效三方协作机制，特别是在K-12教育场景下，如何平衡AI辅助与人工指导成为关键问题。

Method: 开发了三方协作系统支持K-12写作学习，建立了基于系统功能语言学的多维度评估框架和反馈轨迹追踪管道；收集了为期两年的大规模实证数据集，包含来自120所学校10,195名学生的57,954篇作文。

Result: 验证了该系统在提升写作质量方面的有效性：LLM作为生成引擎减轻教师倦怠，教师作为教学把关者和桥梁保证反馈质量；发现存在天花板效应，过度语言扩展会产生边际效用递减；LLM和教师对技能提升都很关键，但需要动态适应的协作方式。

Conclusion: 研究表明LLM-教师协作应随学生熟练度提高而动态调整，需要采用战略性的劳动分工策略，在利用LLM减轻教师负担的同时，确保教师的教学指导作用，避免过度依赖语言扩展带来的收益递减。

Abstract: The double-edged sword of integrating Large Language Models (LLMs) requires an effective triadic collaboration mechanism among LLMs, teachers and students, especially for K-12 education. By developing a triadic collaboration system to support K-12 writing learning, a multidimensional evaluation framework grounded in Systemic Functional Linguistics and the suggestion trajectory tracing pipeline, this paper contributes a large-scale empirical dataset involving $57,954$ essays from $10,195$ students across $120$ schools over two years. Our findings confirm the efficacy of this system in improving writing quality through a strategic labor division: the LLM serves as a generative engine to mitigate teacher burnout, and the teacher acts as a pedagogical gatekeeper and bridge to guarantee feedback quality. While both LLM and teacher are critical for skill improvement, we uncover a ceiling effect where excessive linguistic expansion yields diminishing marginal utility. These suggest a dynamically adaptive LLM-teacher collaboration as student proficiency increases.

</details>


### [317] [Persona Conditioning of Brand Recommendations in Retrieval-Augmented Commercial Chat: A Prominence-Stratified Cross-Provider Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30207)
*Will Jack,Noah Lehman,Keller Maloney,Sarah Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用户画像变化会显著改变AI助手的品牌推荐结果。类目领导者品牌在不同画像下保持约80%一致性，而中端品牌推荐变化高达75%。测量AI品牌感知时必须考虑用户画像差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相同提示词在不同用户语境下（如个体创业者、企业VP、英国中小企业主）会到达AI助手，研究旨在审计这种语境变化如何重塑模型的品牌推荐。现有的AI品牌感知测量方法忽略了用户画像差异，导致系统性偏差。

Method: 采用2000次运行的大规模审计实验，覆盖10个画像×8个提示词×3种模型配置×N=10次重复的设计空间。通过在用户消息前添加画像前缀，使用Jaccard相似度比较推荐集相似性，并以聚集95%置信区间进行统计检验。对比了OpenAI和Anthropic两种模型的推荐行为。

Result: 添加画像前缀后，推荐集相似度（Jaccard）相比同画像基线下降Δ = -0.12至-0.20，所有三个测量单元的聚集95%置信区间均排除零。效应呈现显著分层：类目领导者品牌在不同画像间保持约80%的品牌一致性，而中端品牌随画像变化更换高达75%的推荐集。Anthropic模型效应点估计值大于OpenAI配置，且表现出更高的检索无归因生成率（43-52% vs OpenAI的8-29%）。

Conclusion: 任何AI品牌感知测量都必须以提供查询的买家画像为条件：相同提示词根据模型认为的提问者身份产生实质性不同的推荐集，跨画像聚合的测量协议系统性地掩盖了这种变异。效应集中于中端市场，并在最依赖先验的生成路径上最强，符合模型越依赖训练数据先验和丰富上下文整合时画像响应性越强这一规律。

Abstract: The same prompt -- "best CRM software" -- reaches AI assistants from buyers in widely different contexts: a solo founder, an enterprise VP, a UK SMB owner. We audit how strongly that contextual variation reshapes which brands the model recommends. The audit samples 2,000 runs over a design space of 10 personas x 8 prompts x 3 model configurations x N=10 reps, with the two OpenAI cells at full 8-prompt coverage and the Anthropic sonnet-4.6 / low cell at 4-prompt coverage. Prefixing the user message with a persona drops the recommendation-set similarity (Jaccard) by Delta = -0.12 to -0.20 relative to a same-persona baseline (clustered 95% CIs exclude zero on all three measured cells; the sonnet cell's CI rests on only 4 prompt clusters and is correspondingly wider). The effect is sharply prominence-stratified: category leaders are persona-resistant (~80% same-brand consistency across personas), but mid-market brands swap up to 75% of the recommendation set as the persona changes. The Anthropic model shows a larger point-estimate effect than the OpenAI configurations, though clustered CIs overlap for the closer contrast (sonnet vs. OpenAI/high); the asymmetry is consistent with Anthropic's more retrieval-unattributed generation route (43-52% recommendations without observed retrieval-layer evidence, vs OpenAI's 8-29%, documented in Jack 2026). Any measurement of AI brand perception must condition on the buyer persona supplying the query: the same prompt produces materially different recommendation sets depending on who the model thinks is asking, and a measurement protocol that aggregates across personas systematically obscures that variation. The effect concentrates at mid-market and is largest on the most priors-reliant generation route in our audit, consistent with persona responsiveness growing as models lean more on training-data priors and richer context integration.

</details>


### [318] [When Should Models Change Their Minds? Contextual Belief Management in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30219)
*Haoming Xu,Weihong Xu,Zongrui Li,Mengru Wang,Yunzhi Yao,Chiyu Wu,Jin Shang,Yu Gong,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文信念管理（CBM）任务与BeliefTrack基准，发现大语言模型在长期交互中存在严重的状态维护失败，而强化学习与表征级干预可显著降低失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期交互场景要求语言模型有效管理累积信息，即决定何时更新状态、何时保持状态、以及忽略哪些内容。这一挑战被形式化为上下文信念管理问题：在维持与形式证据对齐的预测信念状态的同时，隔离与任务无关的噪声。

Method: 提出BeliefTrack基准，包含规则发现和电路诊断两个闭世界任务，通过有限信念空间和符号验证器实现精确的回合级评估。定义三种失败类型：失败保持、失败更新、失败隔离。测试多种LLM，比较普通模型、显式信念追踪提示、以及基于信念状态奖励的强化学习方法的表现，并进一步通过探针分析潜在信念状态动态，采用表征级引导进行干预。

Result: 普通LLM表现出严重的CBM失败，显式信念追踪提示仅带来有限提升。基于信念状态奖励的强化学习平均降低失败率70.9%，表征级引导在两个任务中平均降低失败率46.1%。探针分析揭示了失败背后的潜在信念状态动态。

Conclusion: 研究表明LLM在长期交互中存在系统性信念状态管理缺陷，通过强化学习训练与表征空间干预可有效改善，为长上下文场景下的模型能力提升提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Long-horizon interactions require language models to manage accumulating information: when to update their state, when to preserve their state, and what to ignore. We study this challenge as \textbf{Contextual Belief Management (CBM)}: maintaining a predicted belief state aligned with formal evidence while isolating task-irrelevant noise. To make CBM measurable, we introduce BeliefTrack, a closed-world benchmark spanning Rule Discovery and Circuit Diagnosis, where a finite belief space and symbolic verifiers enable exact turn-level evaluation. BeliefTrack diagnoses three failures: Failed Stay, Failed Update, and Failed Isolation. Across multiple LLMs, vanilla models exhibit severe CBM failures, while explicit belief-tracking prompts provide limited gains. In contrast, reinforcement learning with belief-state rewards reduces failure rates by 70.9\% on average. Further probing reveals latent belief-state dynamics behind these failures, and representation-level steering reduces failure rates by 46.1\% across two tasks\footnote{Code is coming soon at https://github.com/zjunlp/CBM.

</details>


### [319] [mcp-proto-okn: Natural-language access to open scientific knowledge graphs through the Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30283)
*Peter W. Rose,Benjamin M. Good,Amanda M. Saravia-Butler,Charlotte A. Nelson,James P. Balhoff,Yaphet Kebede,Patricia L. Whetzel,Christopher Bizon,Andrew I. Su,Sergio E. Baranzini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MCP Server Proto-OKN是一个基于Python的模型上下文协议服务器，使AI助手能够通过自然语言发现、检查、查询和集成科学知识图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低生物医学和科学用户进行跨领域知识图谱分析的门槛，使AI助手能够通过自然语言界面与科学知识图谱进行交互。

Method: 使用FastMCP框架在Python中实现，提供图路由、模式检查、SPARQL执行、本体扩展、多图查询和转录生成等功能。

Result: 成功开发了一个功能完整的MCP服务器，支持通过自然语言进行科学知识图谱的发现、检查、查询和集成，代码已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: 该工具有效降低了跨领域知识图谱分析的技术门槛，为生物医学和科学用户提供了一种便捷的知识图谱交互方式。

Abstract: MCP Server Proto-OKN (mcp-proto-okn) is a Python-based Model Context Protocol server that enables AI assistants to discover, inspect, query and integrate scientific knowledge graphs through natural language. The server provides graph routing, schema inspection, SPARQL execution, ontology expansion, multi-graph querying, and transcript generation, lowering the barrier to cross-domain knowledge graph analysis for biomedical and scientific users.
  mcp-proto-okn is implemented in Python using the FastMCP framework and is available at https://github.com/sbl-sdsc/mcp-proto-okn. Documentation, client configuration instructions, and example analysis transcripts are provided in the GitHub repository.

</details>


### [320] [ProjectionBench: Evaluating Scientific Hypothesis Generation in LLMs Under Progressive Information Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30284)
*A. J. Lew,Y. Cao,M. J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个渐进式基准框架，用于评估大语言模型在科学发现和推理任务中的创新能力与推理能力，并对GPT-5、GPT-5.4、Gemini 2.5 pro和Gemini 3.1 pro进行了评估，发现GPT-5.4在最小信息下仍能保持0.7的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基准主要测试大语言模型的多跳检索能力，但对其科学发现所必需的创新推理能力缺乏评估。科学发现本质上是一个创造性和不确定的过程，需要超越已知知识的推理，因此需要一个新的框架来系统评估模型在科学推理和发现方面的能力。

Method: 构建了一个渐进式评估框架，模型最初只接收论文主题和研究问题，随后逐步揭示技术细节。在每个信息披露阶段，模型需要生成解决研究问题的假设，通过与原论文结论进行原子声明的语义相似度自动评估，测量语义分歧程度。该框架从最小信息到完整实验细节，评估模型的创新性和推理能力。

Result: 在涵盖生物活性材料、机械材料和纳米材料的45篇论文上评估了GPT-5、GPT-5.4、Gemini 2.5 pro和Gemini 3.1 pro。结果表明GPT-5.4和Gemini 3.1 pro表现优于前代模型，其中GPT-5.4在最小上下文条件下仍能保持与真实结论0.7的F1分数一致性。

Conclusion: 该框架为系统评估大语言模型的科学推理和发现能力奠定了基础，对于推动下一代AI科学家/协作者系统的发展至关重要，为未来科学研究辅助工具的开发提供了评估标准。

Abstract: Scientific discovery is an inherently creative and uncertain process, requiring reasoning beyond the recall of known knowledge. While many benchmarks have been proposed to evaluate large language model (LLM) performance on deep research tasks via multi-hop retrieval, their innovative reasoning abilities essential for true scientific discovery remain largely untested. We introduce a benchmark framework for evaluating model performance in scientific discovery and reasoning, building up from a raw problem to the classical null hypothesis test. In our framework, models initially receive only the topic and research question from a recent paper, with technical details progressively revealed. At each stage of information disclosure, the model is tasked with generating hypotheses that address the research question, which is compared with the conclusions from the original paper and evaluated via automated semantic similarity of constituent atomic claims. This progressive evaluation of semantic divergence from ground-truth conclusions enables assessment of a model's innovativeness (under minimal information) to grounded reasoning capabilities (under full experimental details), both critical for using LLMs for scientific discovery purposes. Our framework provides a foundation for systematically evaluating scientific reasoning and discovery capabilities in LLMs, crucial for advancing the development of next-generation AI scientist/co-scientist systems. Specifically, here we evaluate GPT-5, GPT-5.4, Gemini 2.5 pro, and Gemini 3.1 pro preview across 45 papers spanning bioactive materials, mechanical materials, and nanomaterials. We find that GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3.1 pro outperform their previous generation counterparts as expected, and GPT-5.4 in particular maintains 0.7 F1 score alignment with ground truth conclusions even under minimal context.

</details>


### [321] [MIRA: Mid-training Rubric Anchoring for Source-Aware Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30288)
*Haowen Wang,Yaxin Du,Jian Yang,Jiajun Wu,Shukai Liu,Yuxuan Zhang,Pingjie Wang,Siheng Chen,Tuney Zheng,Ming Zhou,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MIRA框架，一种基于自锚定规则发现的源感知数据筛选方法，在代码导向的中期训练中使用一半token即达到全量数据训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中期训练的数据选择面临独特挑战：需要在近预训练规模下优化数据，但数据来源于具有不同格式和训练角色的异构源，需要同时具备可扩展性和源自适应语义标准。现有方法存在不匹配：基于模型的方法可扩展但仅提供隐式质量信号，语义选择方法提供强判断但通常假设固定规则或标准化数据格式。

Method: 提出MIRA（源感知过滤框架），核心思想是将规则构建纳入数据选择流程。MIRA首先为每个源组发现应该评估的内容，然后将这些判断蒸馏为可扩展的学生评分器用于全语料库过滤。该方法包含两个关键步骤：自锚定规则发现和学生评分器蒸馏。

Result: 在包含21个数据源和5个源组的代码导向中期训练实验中，MIRA在九个代码基准测试上超越了数据选择基线方法，仅使用一半的token就匹配了全量语料库训练的性能表现。

Conclusion: MIRA成功解决了中期训练中数据选择的可扩展性与语义判断之间的矛盾，证明了源感知的自适应规则发现在异构数据筛选中的有效性，为现代LLM中期训练阶段的数据选择提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Mid-training has become an important stage in modern LLM development, using large-scale curated mixtures to strengthen capabilities before final post-training. Its data selection problem is distinct: the data are optimized under a pretraining-style objective at near-pretraining scale, but are curated toward downstream capabilities and drawn from heterogeneous sources with different formats and training roles. As a result, effective selection requires both scalability and source-adaptive semantic criteria. Existing model-based methods scale well, but provide only implicit quality signals. Semantic selection methods offer stronger judgments, but usually assume fixed rubrics or standardized data formats. To address this mismatch, we propose MIRA, a source-aware filtering framework based on self-anchored rubric discovery. The key idea is to make rubric construction part of data selection: MIRA first discovers what should be evaluated for each source group, then distills those judgments into scalable student scorers for full-corpus filtering. On code-oriented mid-training with 21 sources and 5 source groups, MIRA outperforms selection baselines across nine code benchmarks and matches the full-corpus run while using only half the tokens.

</details>


### [322] [Demystifying Data Organization for Enhanced LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30334)
*Yalun Dai,Yangyu Huang,Tongshen Yang,Yonghan Wang,Xin Zhang,Wenshan Wu,Qihao Zhao,Hao Li,Yuanyuan Gao,Kim-Hui Yap,Scarlett Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出四种数据组织优化准则（边界锐化、循环调度、课程连续性、局部多样性），并基于此设计了STR和SAW两种数据排序方法，以最小额外计算开销显著提升LLM训练的稳定性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的训练效率严重依赖有效的数据治理。虽然数据选择已被广泛研究，但针对增强训练效果的数据组织策略仍是一个未被充分探索的领域，特别是考虑到当前LLM通常仅训练一个或少数几个epoch，数据顺序的组织对训练效果有重要影响。

Method: 本文系统探索数据组织对LLM训练的影响，通过复用原本为数据效率生成的预计算样本级分数，几乎不产生额外计算开销。作者识别并形式化了四个关键准则：边界锐化（Boundary Sharpening）、循环调度（Cyclic Scheduling）、课程连续性（Curriculum Continuity）和局部多样性（Local Diversity）。基于这些准则，提出了两种新的数据排序方法：STR（Score-based Temperature-scaled Reordering）和SAW（Score-based Alternating Window）。

Result: 在不同模型规模和数据规模上进行了广泛实验，涵盖预训练和SFT（监督微调）阶段，验证了所总结准则的有效性。实验证明所提出的数据排序方法在增强LLM训练稳定性和性能方面表现出良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 数据组织对LLM训练效果具有显著影响，遵循边界锐化、循环调度、课程连续性和局部多样性四个准则可以有效优化训练过程。本文提出的STR和SAW方法能够在几乎不增加计算成本的前提下，提升模型训练的稳定性和最终性能，为LLM训练中的数据组织策略提供了系统性指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized various fields, yet their training efficiency is heavily reliant on effective data curation. While data selection has been widely studied, the strategic data organization for enhanced training remains an underexplored area, particularly since current LLMs are often trained for only one or a few epochs. This paper systematically explores the influence of data organization on LLM training by reusing pre-computed sample-level scores originally generated for data efficiency, thereby incurring minimal additional computational overhead. We identify and formalize four key guidelines for optimizing data organization: Boundary Sharpening, Cyclic Scheduling, Curriculum Continuity, and Local Diversity. Guided by them, we introduce two novel data ordering methods termed STR and SAW. Extensive experiments across different model scales and data sizes, encompassing both pre-training and SFT stages, validate the effectiveness of our summarized guidelines. They also demonstrate the robustness of our proposed data ordering methods in enhancing the stability and performance of LLM training. Github Link: https://github.com/microsoft/data-efficacy/

</details>


### [323] [Locally Coherent, Globally Incoherent: Bounding Compositional Incoherence in Multi-Component LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30335)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多组件LLM代理存在局部一致但全局不一致的问题，导致违反概率公理；本文提出形式化方法度量该问题，并提出了确定性修复方案，但三种直观缓解策略均失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组件LLM代理系统中，各组件只能看到联合问题的部分，即使每个组件局部连贯，组合后仍可能违反基本概率公理。这种局部连贯但全局不连贯的失效模式需要被形式化度量和解决。

Method: 1）形式化定义组合残差eps*（组合结果到联合连贯多面体的L2距离），可从系统输出和跨组件耦合约束运行时计算；2）建立产品结构二分法刻画局部连贯充分条件；3）提出分层Boyle-Dykstra投影进行确定性修复；4）设计anytime-valid e-process进行序列连贯监控。

Result: 在四LLM中等面板的1,876个集合团上，33-94%的团eps*>0，1,770个已解决赌注上每注产生+0.115 nats遗憾；Rayleigh商预测在四类关系中的三类上与观测残差误差在7%以内。三种LLM侧缓解策略（检索、分区感知提示、聚合器LLM）均失效或退步。

Conclusion: 多组件LLM代理的概率组合存在系统性缺陷，局部连贯无法保证全局连贯；本文提出的度量和修复方法有效，但直观的LLM侧缓解策略不可行，需要更深入的架构性解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-component LLM agents assemble probabilistic claims from components that each see only part of a joint problem; the composition can violate basic probability axioms even when every component is locally coherent. We formalise this locally coherent, globally incoherent failure via the compositional residual eps*, the L2 distance from the composed quote to the joint coherent polytope, computable at runtime from system output and the declared cross-component coupling constraints. A product-structure dichotomy characterises when local coherence suffices, and a Rayleigh-quotient prediction matches the observed residual within 7% on three of four relation classes. A hierarchical Boyle-Dykstra projection repairs the composition deterministically; an anytime-valid e-process gives sequential coherence monitoring. Across 1,876 ensemble cliques on a four-LLM mid-tier panel (frontier-panel rerun in Section 5.5), eps* > 0 on 33-94% of cliques, translating to +0.115 nats per bet of regret on 1,770 resolved bets under the proportional allocation rule (the gain collapses to +0.006 under bettors that themselves coherentise). Three intuitive LLM-side mitigations(retrieval, partition-aware prompting, aggregator-LLM) each fail or regress.

</details>


### [324] [Tiny but Trusted: Efficient Vision-Language Reasoning for Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30344)
*Xiaona Zhou,Muntasir Wahed,Tianjiao Yu,Constantin Brif,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 构建了带有高质量异常解释的VisAnomBench基准，基于此微调出的VisAnomReasoner模型在时间序列异常检测任务上显著优于所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型在序列数据异常检测任务上表现不佳，且公共异常检测基准通常只提供区间标注而缺乏自然语言解释，难以微调VLMs产生有依据、可解释的决策。

Method: 构建VisAnomBench基准，从公共时间序列数据集中筛选数据，并使用细粒度、任务特定的奖励从多个大型VLMs中选择高质量的异常解释进行增强；在此基础上通过参数高效微调开发VisAnomReasoner模型。

Result: 在VisAnomBench上，VisAnomReasoner在精确率和F1上分别提升至少21.23和23.87个百分点；在TSB-AD-U基准上的跨基准泛化实验中，精确率和F1分别提升9.57和13.39个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过构建带有高质量解释的基准数据集并进行参数高效微调，可以显著提升视觉语言模型在时间序列异常检测任务上的定位精度和泛化能力，证明了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved impressive performance across many tasks, yet prior studies report unsatisfactory performance when applying large language or multimodal models to finding abnormal patterns in sequential data. Public anomaly detection benchmarks typically provide interval annotations but not natural-language rationales, making it difficult to fine-tune VLMs to produce grounded, interpretable decisions. To address this gap, we construct VisAnomBench, a curated benchmark built from public time-series datasets and augmented with high-quality anomaly explanations selected from multiple large VLMs using fine-grained, task-specific rewards. Through fine-tuning on this benchmark, we develop VisAnomReasoner, a parameter-efficient VLM for time-series anomaly detection. Experimental results on VisAnomBench show that VisAnomReasoner achieves more accurate anomaly localization and consistently outperforms all baselines, with improvements of at least 21.23 and 23.87 percentage points in precision and F1, respectively. Additional experiments on the TSB-AD-U benchmark demonstrate strong cross-benchmark generalization, with VisAnomReasoner improving precision and F1 by 9.57 and 13.39 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [325] [SchGen: PCB Schematic Generation with Semantic-Grounded Code Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30345)
*Qinpei Luo,Ruichun Ma,Xinyu Zhang,Lili Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SchGen，首个能够从自然语言需求生成可编辑PCB原理图的大语言模型，通过引入语义驱动的代码表示和大规模数据集，显著提升了线路连接准确性和功能正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCB原理图设计几乎定义了所有电子硬件，但目前仍主要依赖手工操作，需要大量专业知识。尽管生成式AI在数字和模拟IC设计方面已取得进展，但从自然语言意图生成PCB原理图的研究仍处于空白状态。现有原理图格式存在冗长、工具特定语法和几何描述过重的问题，难以可靠生成。

Method: 提出了一种语义驱动的代码表示方法，将原理图编辑原语与相对位置和基于引脚名的布线编码，将几何驱动的生成问题转化为语义驱动的匹配任务。同时通过人机协作流水线构建了大规模的PCB原理图与用户提示配对数据集，将开源硬件设计转换为所提出的表示形式。

Result: SchGen在线路连接准确性和功能正确性方面显著优于替代表示方法和更大的通用大语言模型。实验证明了表示设计在复杂硬件设计任务中启用生成模型的关键作用。

Conclusion: 表示设计对于复杂硬件设计任务的生成式模型具有关键作用。通过将几何驱动的生成问题转化为语义驱动的匹配任务，使得LLM能够有效处理PCB原理图生成，填补了该研究领域的空白。

Abstract: Printed circuit board (PCB) schematic design defines nearly all electronic hardware, but it remains manual and expertise-intensive. While generative AI has advanced digital and analog IC design, PCB schematic generation from natural-language intent is largely unexplored. This paper presents SchGen, the first large language model that generates editable PCB schematics from natural-language requests. The key challenge lies in the lack of an LLM-suited representation and a large-scale dataset. Current schematic formats are dominated by verbose, tool-specific syntax and geometry-heavy descriptions, making them difficult to generate reliably. We introduce a semantically grounded code representation that encodes schematic editing primitives with relative placement and pin-name-based wiring, transforming a geometry-driven generation problem into a semantics-driven matching task amenable to LLMs. We further construct a large-scale dataset of PCB schematics paired with user prompts via a human-agent collaborative pipeline that converts open-source hardware designs into our representation. Experiments show that SchGen significantly outperforms alternative representations and even larger general-purpose LLMs on wire connectivity accuracy and functional correctness. Our results highlight the critical role of representation design in enabling generative models for complex hardware design tasks.

</details>


### [326] [Physics Is All You Need? A Case Study in Physicist-Supervised AI Development of Scientific Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30353)
*Nhat-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过案例研究分析了AI编程代理在物理代码开发中的角色定位、局限性及监督策略，指出当前AI代理易陷入局部优化陷阱，其可信产出严重依赖监督设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI代理在科研编程任务中究竟扮演工具、协作者还是研究者的角色，明确其能力边界与监督必要性。

Method: 对一名物理学家监督AI编程代理（Claude Code）进行为期12天、57次会话的量化案例研究，构建JAX可微扰论模块CLAX-PT，记录并分类15次监督干预事件。

Result: 代理自主解决10个问题，依赖物理知识解决2个；剩余3个未被测试检测的问题具有共性——代理将症状缓解误判为根本原因解决。其在一个无法表征目标物理的架构上浪费33次会话调整参数，且提交了通过测试但无理论依据的错误修正。三种关键监督实践可捕捉测试遗漏：多样化参数点测试、跨会话变更日志共享、禁止非物理数值补丁的规则。

Conclusion: 监督设计而非模型能力决定了代理产出的可信度。当前代理缺乏提出架构替代方案及区分预测准确性与解释正确性的能力，仅靠模型规模扩展难以弥补此差距。

Abstract: Are AI agents tools, co-authors, or researchers? We present a quantified case study ($N=1$): a physicist supervising an AI coding agent (Claude Code, Sonnet and Opus models) over 12 work days and 57 sessions to build CLAX-PT, a differentiable one-loop perturbation theory module in JAX. We documented and classified 15 supervision events by intervention level.
  The agent resolved ten autonomously by iterating against oracle tests. Two more by the physicist's domain knowledge. The three it could not -- all evaded oracle detection -- share a common property: the agent treated symptom reduction as root-cause resolution. It spent 33 of the 57 sessions adjusting coefficients within a code architecture that could not represent the target physics, and could not re-evaluate its CLASS-PT branch choice even when prompted to reconsider; only an injected physics concept (anisotropic BAO damping) triggered the redesign. Separately, the agent committed a calibrated correction that passed all oracle tests but corresponded to no quantity in the theory, predicting wrong values at any other cosmology.
  The fudge factor was caught and replaced within the same session. Three supervision practices proved critical for catching what oracle tests missed: testing at diverse parameter points beyond the fiducial calibration; shared changelogs that surfaced stalled exploration across sessions; and an explicit rule against unphysical numerical patches. In this case, supervision design, not model capability, determined whether the agent's output was trustworthy. Closing the gap would require agents that propose architectural alternatives rather than optimize within a given structure, and distinguish predictive adequacy from explanatory correctness -- capabilities not exhibited here, not obviously addressed by scaling alone. [Abridged.]

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [327] [Constant Depth Threshold Circuits For Exhaustive Epistasis Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29719)
*André Ribeiro,Aleksandar Ilic,Leonel Sousa*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于阈值门的神经形态电路，用于高效解决生物信息学中的上位性检测问题，实现了运行时仅受组合数量约束且无额外复杂度开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模神经形态硬件的发展使得阈值门电路的实际实现成为可能，而传统计算方法在处理生物信息学中最复杂的组合问题之一——上位性检测时面临巨大计算挑战，这促使作者探索利用神经形态硬件的计算优势来解决这一难题。

Method: 设计了专用电路以流水线方式高效计算所有数据集组合的相对频率，利用协同定位的内存和可配置并行性；提出了使用泄漏积分发放（LIF）神经元的数据编码和组合生成策略，为恒定深度的阈值门种群计数电路提供输入；通过组合无界扇入恒定深度阈值门电路执行种群计数和二进制数组求和。

Result: 获得了对数级深度和log-三次线性连接的种群计数电路；运行时仅受计算组合数量的约束，没有额外的复杂度开销；使用对数线性空间，相比传统方法实现了复杂度优势。

Conclusion: 通过考虑典型硬件特性（如有限扇入和可变精度），成功构建了适用于神经形态硬件的高效电路，为解决复杂的上位性检测问题提供了新的计算范式，展示了阈值门电路在处理复杂组合问题方面的优势。

Abstract: The development of large-scale neuromorphic hardware has made practical implementations of threshold gate-based circuits a near-term possibility. The complexity advantages regarding traditional computing classes, as evidenced in the literature, have prompted us to tackle Epistasis Detection, one of the most computationally complex combinatorial problems in bioinformatics. We propose specially designed circuits that calculate the relative frequencies of all dataset combinations in an efficient pipelined fashion, taking advantage of co-located memory and configurable parallelism, obtaining complexity gains. Overall, we obtain the runtime to be bounded by the number of combinations to calculate, without any additional complexity overhead, contrary to classical approaches, using log-linear space. To accomplish this, we propose a data encoding and combination generation strategy using Leaky Integrate and Fire (LIF) neurons, that feeds a constant depth threshold gate population count circuit. Accounting for typical hardware characteristics, such as limited fan-in and variable precisions, we obtain logarithmic depth and log-cubic linear connections, for the population count circuit by composing developed unbounded fan-in constant depth threshold gate circuits to perform population count and binary array sum.

</details>


### [328] [Design-Oriented Modeling of TSV Substrate Noise Coupling to Ring VCOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29867)
*Ilias Exouzidis,Alberto Garcia-Ortiz,George Floros,Georgios Panagopoulos*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向设计的TSV衬底噪声评估方法，建立了三端口RLGC宏模型，并通过22nm FD-SOI工艺的环形VCO验证了TSV噪声对射频电路频谱纯度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅通孔（TSV）在3D-IC和芯粒系统中实现高密度垂直互连，但其金属-氧化物-硅结构引入显著的寄生耦合路径，可能降低敏感射频模块的频谱纯度，因此需要一种紧凑、面向设计的方法来评估TSV引起的衬底噪声。

Method: 推导了信号-地TSV对的闭式解析三端口RLGC宏模型，显式暴露衬底节点；采用22nm FD-SOI工艺设计三级环形VCO，利用PDK中的射频器件直接访问晶体管衬底终端进行可控噪声注入；使用Spectre RF的多音谐波平衡仿真量化TSV干扰源对振荡器输出频谱的影响。

Result: 1GHz、0.5Vpp的干扰源导致主边带杂散为-35.2 dBc；边带杂散幅度随干扰源幅度单调增加；频率扫描显示低通耦合响应，500MHz时杂散为-20.2 dBc，2GHz时降至-33.1 dBc。

Conclusion: 所提出的紧凑设计方法能够有效评估TSV引起的衬底噪声，揭示了TSV干扰对RF电路频谱纯度的量化影响规律，为3D-IC混合信号电路设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Through-silicon vias (TSVs) enable dense vertical interconnects in 3D-IC and chiplet systems, but their metal-oxide-silicon structure introduces significant parasitic coupling paths that can degrade the spectral purity of sensitive RF blocks. This paper presents a compact, design-oriented methodology for assessing TSV-induced substrate noise in mixed-signal circuits. We derive a closed-form analytical three-port RLGC macromodel for a Signal-Ground TSV pair that explicitly exposes the substrate node. The methodology is validated using a three-stage Ring VCO designed in a 22 nm FD-SOI technology, where specific RF devices from the process design kit (PDK) provide direct access to the transistor substrate terminals for controlled noise injection. Multi-tone Harmonic Balance simulations in Spectre RF quantify the impact of TSV aggressors on the oscillator's output spectrum. The results indicate that an aggressor of 1 GHz, 0.5 V$_{pp}$ induces a primary sideband spur of -35.2 dBc. Sensitivity characterization reveals that the magnitude of these sideband spurs increases monotonically with the aggressor amplitude. Furthermore, frequency sweeps demonstrate a low-pass coupling response, where the induced spur magnitude decreases from -20.2 dBc at 500 MHz to -33.1 dBc at 2 GHz.

</details>


### [329] [Precomputed 1D-CNNs for Atrial Fibrillation Detection on Tiny Smart Sensor Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29994)
*Lukas Einhaus,Natalie Maman,Julian Hoever,Andreas Erbslöh,Gregor Schiele*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分组卷积的新型卷积块和超参数选择算法，用于改进FPGA上LUT预计算技术的可扩展性，实现了高效的时间序列分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUT预计算技术虽然能实现高资源效率和超低延迟的神经网络FPGA实现，但存在可扩展性差的问题。现有工作使用深度可分离卷积改进可扩展性，但仍需进一步优化。

Method: 将深度可分离卷积方法推广到更一般的分组卷积形式，提出一种新型卷积块结构，并设计了指导该模块超参数选择的算法。

Result: 在MIT-BIH心电数据集上预测房颤的任务中，实现的硬件加速器仅需2,844个LUT，无需DSP或BRAM即可部署在AMD Spartan 7 S15上，F1分数达到95%。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功改善了LUT预计算技术的可扩展性，在保持高精度的同时实现了极其节省资源的FPGA部署，适用于智能传感器系统的时间序列分析。

Abstract: 1D-CNNs play a crucial role for time-series analysis on tiny smart sensor systems, e.g. for biosignal analysis, predictive maintenance, or structural health monitoring. LUTbased precomputation has emerged as an interesting optimization technique to implement such neural networks on FPGAs. The core idea is to precompute all possible outputs of a neural network layer and store them directly in the lookup tables of the FPGAs. This enables highly resource-efficient networks with ultra-low latency but suffers from poor scalability. Previous work has explored using depthwise-separable convolutions to improve scalability. In this paper, we generalize this approach to consider additional forms of grouped convolutions. Based on this, we propose a novel type of convolutional block and an algorithm to guide the choice of hyper parameters for this block. We evaluate our approach on a medical time-series dataset for predicting atrial fibrillation using the MIT-BIH database (ECG recordings). The resulting hardware accelerators are small enough to be deployed on an AMD Spartan 7 S15. They achieve a F1-Score of up to 95% while only requiring 2,844 LUTs and no DSPs or BRAM.

</details>


### [330] [elasticAI.explorer: Towards a Unified End-to-End Framework for Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30019)
*Natalie Maman,Florian Hettstedt,Andreas Erbslöh,Gregor Schiele*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出elasticAI.explorer，一个基于Optuna的可扩展硬件感知神经架构搜索框架，通过YAML定义搜索空间，支持硬件在环NAS工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NAS框架将搜索空间定义、模型实现和部署管道紧密耦合，难以扩展到新硬件平台和自定义算子，工程开销大。

Method: 构建基于Optuna的可扩展Python框架；引入YAML格式搜索空间规范，动态转换为可执行神经网络模型；支持层级式、单元式和分层搜索空间；集成硬件特定代码生成、Docker跨编译工具链和设备端基准测试二进制文件自动创建；提供FLOPs、参数量和延迟评估器。

Result: 实现了统一的优化和部署接口，支持硬件在环NAS工作流程，降低了嵌入式AI部署的工程开销，加速了异构加速器平台的硬件感知NAS研究。

Conclusion: elasticAI.explorer框架有效解决了现有NAS框架的扩展性问题，为硬件感知神经架构搜索提供了灵活、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has become an important approach for automatically designing neural networks under task-specific and hardware-specific constraints. However, many existing NAS frameworks tightly couple search space definitions, model implementations, and deployment pipelines, making extension to new hardware platforms and custom operators difficult. In this paper, we present the elasticAI.explorer, an extensible Python framework for hardware-aware NAS built on top of Optuna. The framework introduces a YAML-based search space specification that dynamically translates into executable neural network models during sampling. The approach supports layer-wise, cell-based, and hierarchical search spaces while maintaining a unified interface for optimization and deployment. Beyond architecture generation, the framework integrates hardware-specific code generation, Docker-based cross-compilation toolchains, and automated creation of on-device benchmarking binaries, enabling hardware-in-the-loop NAS workflows. The system further provides extensible evaluators for FLOPs, parameter count, and latency estimation. The elasticAI.explorer aims to reduce the engineering overhead of embedded AI deployment and accelerate research on hardware-aware NAS for heterogeneous accelerator platforms

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [331] [RTP-LLM: High-Performance Alibaba LLM Inference Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29639)
*Boyu Tan,Jiarui Guo,Zongwei Lv,Hanbo Sun,Tong Yang,Kan Liu,Xinfei Shi,Zetao Hu,Yaxin Yu,Chi Zhang,Jianning Zhang,Xi Yang,Wei Zhang,Bo Cai,Silu Zhou,Xiyu Wang,Na He,Yinghao Yu,Wending Bao,Guiyang Huang,Yuxing Yuan,Juncheng Yin,Nan Wang,Lin Yang,Zechao Zhang,Lu Chen,Guoding Li,Tao Lan,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: RTP-LLM是阿里巴巴开源的高性能大语言模型推理引擎，通过模型加载优化、Prefill-Decode解耦架构、分层KV缓存管理及推测解码等技术，在8B-235B参数规模上实现了显著性能提升，已服务超1亿用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）在实际大规模部署中面临显著挑战，包括模型加载效率低、计算密集的prefill阶段与内存受限的decode阶段相互制约、KV缓存管理效率不足等问题，亟需一个面向工业级规模的高性能推理引擎解决方案。

Method: RTP-LLM采用集成化设计方案：1）通过文件顺序驱动I/O和并行I/O-通信重叠优化模型加载；2）提出Prefill-Decode解耦架构，分离计算密集的prefill与内存受限的decode阶段；3）引入分层多级KV缓存管理实现高效缓存复用；4）集成模块化推测解码、自适应KV缓存量化及解耦多模态处理，支持多级并行。

Result: 在8B-235B参数规模及真实生产负载测试中，RTP-LLM相比vLLM和SGLang实现：模型加载加速4.7x-6.3x；生产流量调度中TTFT P95延迟降低35-37%，缓存复用提升215%；推测解码和多模态推理吞吐分别提升1.12x-2.48x和1.86x-2.52x；量化推理批处理延迟降低35-40%，TTFT提升1.9x-3.0x。

Conclusion: RTP-LLM通过生产验证的架构设计和开源可用性，为大语言模型的工业级部署提供了综合解决方案，已在阿里集团服务超过1亿用户，证明其在实际大规模应用中的有效性和可靠性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized AI applications, but deploying them at scale presents significant challenges. We present RTP-LLM, a high-performance inference engine for industrial-scale LLM deployment, successfully deployed across Alibaba Group serving over 100 million users. RTP-LLM addresses fundamental bottlenecks through integrated design. It optimizes model loading via file-order-driven I/O and parallel I/O-communication overlapping. The Prefill-Decode Disaggregation architecture decouples compute-intensive prefill from memory-bound decode phases, combined with hierarchical multi-tiered KV cache management enabling efficient cache reuse. In addition, RTP-LLM incorporates modular speculative decoding supporting multiple algorithms, adaptive KV cache quantization, and decoupled multimodal processing, with support for multi-level parallelism.
  Comprehensive evaluations across diverse model architectures (8B-235B parameters) have been conducted, where both controlled benchmarks and real production workloads are used. The results demonstrate RTP-LLM's superior performance against vLLM and SGLang: 4.7x-6.3x model loading speedup, 35-37% TTFT P95 latency reduction with 215% cache reuse improvement in production traffic scheduling, 1.12x-2.48x and 1.86x-2.52x throughput improvements in speculative decoding and multimodal inference, respectively, and 35-40% batch latency reduction with 1.9x-3.0x TTFT improvement in quantized inference. RTP-LLM's production-proven architecture and open-source availability make it a comprehensive solution for industrial LLM deployment.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [332] [ReclaimNet: Reclaim-Aware Network Protocols for Voluntary GPU Sharing on Campus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28872)
*Wenyang Jia,Jingjing Wang,Xianneng Zou,Kai Lei*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReclaimNet，一种针对高校分散GPU资源的网络层迁移协议套件，将提供者回收视为一级契约而非故障，通过回收感知检查点调度、波动感知目标选择和截止期感知迁移流量控制三种机制，在54节点测试床上相比Slurm减少66%的工作损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高校校园拥有丰富但分散的GPU资源，其自愿共享受到阻碍，原因在于可撤销的自主所有权与假设静态故障风险、同构互连和无限传输窗口的迁移机制之间存在不匹配。

Method: 提出ReclaimNet网络层迁移协议套件，包含三种机制：(i)回收感知检查点调度，联合适应时变离开风险和共存作业间的竞争带宽；(ii)波动感知目标选择，整合拓扑、存活概率和通知窗口可行性；(iii)截止期感知迁移流量控制，配备边缘执行和亚毫秒级TC BPF开关。

Result: 在54节点异构校园测试床上进行为期两个月的部署，相比Slurm抢占-重队列机制减少66%的工作损失，相比流水线冗余检查点减少38%的工作损失，停机时间缩短38%，对后台研究流量的性能影响低于3%。

Conclusion: ReclaimNet成功解决了校园GPU资源共享中的资源回收问题，显著降低了工作损失和停机时间，同时保持对后台研究流量的低影响，为校园分布式GPU资源的高效利用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: University campuses host abundant but fragmented GPU resources whose voluntary sharing is blocked by a mismatch between revocable, autonomous ownership and migration mechanisms that assume stationary failure hazards, homogeneous interconnects, and unbounded transfer windows. We present ReclaimNet, a network-layer migration protocol suite that treats provider reclaim as a first-class contract rather than a failure case, combining three mechanisms: (i) reclaim-aware checkpoint scheduling that jointly adapts to time-varying departure hazards and contended bandwidth across co-resident jobs; (ii) volatility-aware destination selection integrating topology, survival probability, and notice-window feasibility; and (iii) deadline-aware migration traffic control with edge enforcement and a submillisecond TC BPF kill-switch. A two-month deployment on a 54-node heterogeneous campus testbed reduces work loss by 66% over Slurm preempt-and-requeue and 38% over pipeline-redundancy checkpointing, with 38% shorter downtime and under 3% degradation of background research traffic. The prototype is open-sourced at the anonymous repository https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ICNP2026-ReclaimNet/.

</details>


### [333] [Network Optimization Aspects of Autonomous Vehicles: Challenges and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29518)
*Rudolf Krecht,Tamas Budai,Erno Horvath,Akos Kovacs,Nobert Marko,Miklos Unger*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于联网和自动驾驶车辆（CAVs）网络优化的综述性文章，旨在澄清公众对CAVs的误解，并重点介绍了包括协同感知在内的多学科方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球城市化、人口增长和新兴网络解决方案等大趋势正在加速联网和自动驾驶车辆（CAVs）产业的发展。然而，公众对CAVs的认知中存在许多事实、误解甚至过度期待，因此有必要提供全面的综述以消除误解。

Method: 采用多学科方法进行综述，重点介绍协同感知等技术，并结合相关用例和实验结果进行分析。

Result: 提供了对CAVs网络优化方面的全面回顾，分享了作者基于丰富经验的见解和知识，展示了相关的用例和实验结果。

Conclusion: 消除了关于CAVs的常见误解，勾勒了自动驾驶车辆网络优化方面的未来发展方向。

Abstract: Global megatrends, such as urbanization, population growth, and emerging network solutions are accelerating the development of the Connected and Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs) industry. There are many truths, some misconceptions, and even some excitement about CAVs in the public's opinion. The main objective of the current article is to provide a comprehensive review, eliminate misconceptions, and outline the future of the network optimization aspects of autonomous vehicles by presenting various multidisciplinary methods, such as cooperative perception. Given our extensive experience with CAVs, we are aiming to share some of the insights and knowledge we have gained, along with relevant use-cases and experiment results.

</details>


### [334] [ARIADNE: AI-RAN Informed Link Adaptation in Digital Twin Network Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29772)
*Maria Tsampazi,Neagin Neasamoni Santhi,Nicole Perrotta,Falko Dressler,Tommaso Melodia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出ARIADNE，一个基于强化学习的在线链路自适应模块，集成SIONNA数字孪生平台，在频谱效率上分别超越工业标准和最先进方法11%和20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI赋能的无线接入网络（RAN）受到业界和学术界广泛关注，而数字孪生为AI/ML解决方案提供了安全的实验环境，可在部署到真实RAN前测试在线算法，从而降低物理现场测试的成本和安全风险。

Method: 提出ARIADNE模块，一个基于在线强化学习（RL）的链路自适应方案，与SIONNA数字孪生平台无缝集成，用于执行调制编码方案（MCS）选择。

Result: ARIADNE在频谱效率上分别超越工业标准11%、超越最先进方法20%。RL学习到的MCS选择策略与外环链路自适应（OLLA）不同，根据配置可表现出更保守或更激进的行为，这一趋势在5G空口（OTA）测量数据的离线训练中得到进一步验证。

Conclusion: 基于强化学习的链路自适应方法在数字孪生环境中表现优异，能够学习到与传统的OLLA不同的策略，为AI-RAN研究提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI)-powered Radio Access Network (RAN) networks have attracted significant attention from both industry and academia. Meanwhile, Digital Twins offer a safe playground for experimenting with AI/Machine Learning (ML)-based solutions for advanced AI-RAN research. By enabling the testing of online algorithms before deployment on the RAN, they reduce costs and safety risks associated with physical field testing. In this article, we propose ARIADNE, an online Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based module that seamlessly integrates with SIONNA and is tasked with performing link adaptation. We explore different design choices and demonstrate how ARIADNE can surpass industry-standard and state-of-the-art methods by achieving up to 11% and 20% improvements in Spectral Efficiency, respectively. Finally, we show that RL learns a Modulation and Coding Scheme (MCS) selection strategy that diverges from Outer Loop Link Adaptation (OLLA), exhibiting either more conservative or more aggressive behavior depending on the configuration, a trend further corroborated by training offline on 5th generation (5G) over-the-air (OTA) measurements.

</details>


### [335] [TraceCodec: A Compiler-Backed Neural Codec for Stateful Multi-Flow Network Traffic Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29941)
*Junhui Ding,Xinchen Zhang,Xiaohui Xie,Shinan Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TraceCodec，一种面向多流轨迹的状态感知神经编解码器，通过将数据包提升为定时包动作并学习连续潜变量，解决了现有方法中解码接口将学习行为与协议约束纠缠的根本瓶颈，实现了高保真度PCAP生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键网络工作流需要高保真度数据包捕获（PCAP）用于测试、安全分析和协议验证，而不仅是统计流级摘要。现有数据包生成器直接解码到原始数据包字段，该接口将学习的行为选择与确定性协议后果纠缠在一起，迫使数据包实现依赖事后启发式修复。作者识别出这种解码接口是根本瓶颈。

Method: 提出TraceCodec，一种状态感知神经编解码器。它将每个数据包提升为带有显式流槽和传输线索的定时包动作，然后学习连续的逐包潜变量。一个确定性编译器将解码后的动作降低回PCAP，负责端点分配、TCP状态、合法性约束和数据包渲染。潜变量层暴露了一个面向生成器的序列空间，使下游流量模型能够在包动作潜变量而非原始头部字段上操作。

Result: 在CICIDS2017 Monday数据集上，TraceCodec将数据包计数、协议组成和流群体匹配到0.03%以内。在相同非修复策略下，原始字段基线使流计数和TCP状态产生了数量级的扭曲。结构诊断表明，TraceCodec保留了原始字段解码器所碎片的TCP状态转换和多流交错。

Conclusion: 这项工作为高保真度数据包轨迹生成建立了新的基础，证明了通过状态感知编解码器和解耦学习行为与协议约束，可以显著提升PCAP生成的保真度。

Abstract: Critical networking workflows require high-fidelity packet captures (PCAPs) for testing, security analysis, and protocol validation, not just statistical flow-level summaries. Recent packet generators have demonstrated protocol-constrained PCAP synthesis, but they universally decode directly to raw packet fields. That interface entangles learned behavioral choices with deterministic protocol consequences, which forces packet realization to depend on post-hoc heuristic repair. We identify this decode interface as the fundamental bottleneck and present TraceCodec, a state-aware neural codec for stateful multi-flow traces. TraceCodec lifts each packet into a timed packet action with explicit flow slots and transport cues, then learns a continuous per-packet latent. A deterministic compiler lowers decoded actions back to PCAPs, owning endpoint assignment, TCP state, legality constraints, and packet rendering. The latent layer exposes a generator-facing sequence space, so downstream traffic models can operate on packet-action latents rather than raw header fields. On CICIDS2017 Monday, TraceCodec matches packet count, protocol composition, and flow population to within 0.03%. Raw-field baselines under the same non-repair policy distort flow counts and TCP state by orders of magnitude. Structural diagnostics show that TraceCodec preserves TCP state transitions and multi-flow interleaving that raw-field decoders fragment. This work establishes a new foundation for high-fidelity packet-trace generation.

</details>


### [336] [Intent-Based Orchestration in Open RAN: An ns-3 Simulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30079)
*Pouya Agheli,Grégoire Lefebvre*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于ns-3的可扩展仿真框架，用于评估Open RAN架构中基于意图的语义感知控制。实验表明，该方法能在适度降低分组投递率和吞吐量的代价下，显著提高意图满意度并减少无线资源使用和计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Open RAN架构的发展，需要一种能够评估基于意图的、语义感知的网络控制方法的仿真框架。现有框架缺乏对外部RAN智能控制器（RIC）组件的集成支持，且难以实现跨不同时间尺度的意图驱动编排，同时维持标准化网络行为。

Method: 构建了一个基于ns-3的可扩展仿真框架，集成了外部RAN智能控制器（RIC）组件，通过内部分布式应用程序（dApps）实现细粒度控制。设计了一个用于无线资源管理（RRM）的基于意图的dApp作为示例用例，利用dApps可用的真实关键性能测量（KPMs）制定调度问题，并引入了新的意图满意度评分（ISS）指标，该指标结合了失真和感知导向的度量来量化意图相关信息的传递。

Result: 仿真结果表明，基于意图的RRM能够提高意图满意度评分（ISS），同时显著减少无线资源使用和计算开销，但代价是分组投递率和吞吐量有适度降低。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于ns-3的仿真框架成功实现了对Open RAN架构中基于意图控制的评估，证明了意图驱动方法在无线资源管理中的可行性，为语义感知的网络控制研究提供了有效的仿真工具和评估方法。

Abstract: This paper presents an extensible ns-3-based simulation framework for evaluating intent-based, semantics-aware control in Open RAN architectures. The framework integrates external Radio Access Network (RAN) Intelligent Controller (RIC) components and supports fine-grained control via internal distributed applications (dApps), enabling intent-based RAN orchestration across different timescales while maintaining standardized network behavior. As an illustrative use case, we implement an intent-based dApp for radio resource management (RRM) under realistic observability constraints. The scheduling problem is formulated using realistic key performance measurements (KPMs) available to dApps, together with a newly introduced Intent Satisfaction Score (ISS), which quantifies the delivery of intent-relevant information by combining distortion- and perception-oriented measures. Simulation results show that intent-based RRM can improve ISS while significantly reducing radio resource usage and computational overhead, at the cost of a moderate reduction in packet delivery ratio and throughput.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [337] [HPC-vQPU: A Service-Export Architecture for Virtual QPUs on Batch-Scheduled HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28845)
*Shusen Liu,Pascal Jahan Elahi,Ugo Varetto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了HPC-vQPU架构，在批处理调度的HPC系统上实现了设备保真的虚拟QPU服务，通过控制平面与执行平面分离以及原子化快照绑定机制，解决了安全超算环境下量子模拟服务化的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备感知的量子模拟越来越需要HPC规模的加速器，但安全的超级计算机暴露的是批处理调度执行环境，而非量子软件所期望的交互式、面向后端的接口。核心障碍不仅是远程作业提交：HPC托管的虚拟QPU必须在队列延迟、调度器分配、计算节点隔离和部分执行侧故障的情况下，保持拓扑、原生门和校准语义，同时不开放进入集群的入站路径。

Method: 提出HPC-vQPU服务导出架构，将云面向的控制平面（负责设备身份、任务生命周期、快照绑定和事件投影）与HPC驻留的执行平面（通过调度器支持的GPU作业认领并执行工作）分离。协调完全通过出站和代理发起的方式进行。核心抽象是在认领时刻原子绑定的拓扑和校准感知设备快照，作为不可变契约带入执行，使每个调度作业保持密闭性同时保留新鲜设备语义。

Result: 在Pawsey超算研究中心基于Setonix GPU、Qiskit-Aer/cuQuantum和IBM Fez校准数据实现了HPC-vQPU。生产实验表明：服务开销有界且可加，工作负载扩展仅限于模拟器；承载校准的快照产生可测量的输出偏移；认领时绑定防止了认领前设备突变后的陈旧执行；并发代理精确一次完成50/50任务；显式恢复在代理故障后恢复陈旧运行任务。

Conclusion: 结果表明，安全的、调度器中介的HPC基础设施可以将设备保真的量子模拟作为交互式虚拟QPU服务导出，为在安全超算环境中提供量子计算服务提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Device-aware quantum simulation increasingly requires HPC-scale accelerators, yet secure supercomputers expose batch-scheduled execution environments rather than the interactive, backend-oriented interfaces expected by quantum software. The key obstacle is not only remote job submission: an HPC-hosted virtual QPU must preserve topology, native-gate, and calibration semantics across queue delay, scheduler allocation, compute-node isolation, and partial execution-side failures, without opening inbound paths into the cluster.
  We present HPC-vQPU, a service-export architecture for virtual QPUs on batch-scheduled HPC systems. HPC-vQPU separates a cloud-facing control plane, which owns device identity, task lifecycle, snapshot binding, and event projection, from an HPC-resident execution plane, which claims work and realises it through scheduler-backed GPU jobs. Coordination is exclusively outbound and agent initiated. The central abstraction is a topology- and calibration-aware device snapshot bound atomically at claim time and carried into execution as an immutable contract, making each scheduled job hermetic while preserving fresh device semantics.
  We implement HPC-vQPU at the Pawsey Supercomputing Research Centre using Setonix GPUs, Qiskit-Aer/cuQuantum, and IBM Fez calibration data. Production experiments show that service overhead is bounded and additive, while workload scaling remains confined to the simulator; calibration-bearing snapshots produce measurable output shifts; claim-time binding prevents stale execution after pre-claim device mutation; concurrent agents complete 50/50 tasks exactly once; and explicit recovery restores stale running tasks after agent failure. These results show that secure, scheduler-mediated HPC infrastructure can export device-faithful quantum simulation as an interactive virtual-QPU service.

</details>


### [338] [Understanding and Reducing Metadata-Driven Host Overheads in Sampling-Based GNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29346)
*Yidong Gong,Saima Afrin,Yuchen Ma,Guannan Wang,Bin Ren,Pradeep Kumar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ZEROGNN通过将元数据驱动的控制循环完全移至GPU端执行，消除了采样型GNN训练中的主机-设备协调开销，实现了高达5.28倍的端到端加速和近100%的GPU执行占比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习工作负载（特别是采样型图神经网络训练）表现出动态的、元数据驱动的执行特征，运行时生成的信息决定了内存分配和内核启动决策。这种行为导致CPU处于关键路径上，引入了持续的主机-设备协调开销和频繁的GPU-CPU同步，在GPU计算量较小时这些开销主导了端到端运行时间。现有的CUDA Graphs和GPU动态并行方法无法解决此问题，因为元数据驱动的控制循环仍然依赖主机中介，且执行结构在迭代间变化。

Method: ZEROGNN提出了一种完全GPU驻留执行的系统方案，包含三个关键技术：(1) 将运行时元数据保持在设备端，避免主机访问；(2) 在固定的启动结构中调解动态执行，通过保守但紧凑的执行包络恢复CUDA Graph的可重放性；(3) 实现与理想元数据感知分配相当的内存效率，同时消除主机端瓶颈以支持强多GPU扩展。

Result: 在采样型GNN工作负载上的实验表明，ZEROGNN实现了：(1) 高达5.28倍的端到端加速；(2) 接近100%的GPU执行占比；(3) 与理想元数据感知分配相当的内存效率；(4) 通过消除主机端瓶颈实现了强劲的多GPU扩展能力。

Conclusion: ZEROGNN成功地将元数据驱动的控制循环从主机端完全转移到GPU端，解决了动态执行模式下主机-设备协调开销的关键问题，为动态深度学习工作负载提供了高效的GPU驻留执行方案，在性能和可扩展性方面都取得了显著突破。

Abstract: Modern deep learning workloads increasingly exhibit dynamic, metadata-driven execution, where runtime-generated information determines memory provisioning and kernel launch decisions. In sampling-based graph neural network (GNN) training, this behavior places the CPU on the critical path, introducing persistent host-device orchestration overhead and frequent GPU-CPU synchronization, which dominate end-to-end runtime when GPU computation is small. Existing approaches, including CUDA Graphs and GPU dynamic parallelism, fail to address this problem because the metadata-driven control loop remains host-mediated, and execution structure varies across iterations. We present ZEROGNN, a system that removes the host from the metadata-driven control loop and enables fully GPU-resident execution under dynamic behavior. ZEROGNN keeps runtime metadata on-device, mediates dynamic execution within a fixed launch structure, and provisions a conservative yet tight execution envelope to restore CUDA Graph replayability. Experiments on sampling-based GNN workloads show that ZEROGNN achieves up to 5.28 x end-to-end speedup, near 100% GPU execution fraction, and memory efficiency comparable to ideal metadata-informed allocation, while enabling strong multi-GPU scaling by eliminating host-side bottlenecks.

</details>


### [339] [Silent Data Corruption Protection through Efficient Task Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29506)
*Mia Reitz,Claudia Fohry*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对集群AMT运行时上嵌套fork-join程序的复制方案，通过记录任务树并在结果不匹配时自顶向下遍历识别受影响任务并重新计算，以检测和纠正静默数据损坏(SDC)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超级计算机集群规模不断增长导致对静默数据损坏(SDC)的敏感性增加，SDC可能使程序结果无效。复制是SDC保护的常见策略，但由于动态任务生成和工作窃取机制，将复制应用于集群上的异步多任务(AMT)运行时具有挑战性，难以识别被复制的任务。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的复制方案：复制计算并记录任务树；当最终结果不匹配时，自顶向下遍历任务树识别所有可能影响最终结果的损坏任务；通过重新计算这些任务进行恢复，同时复用正确子任务的结果。

Result: 在Itoyori集群AMT运行时变体中实现了该方案，实验结果表明识别和重新处理受影响任务的时间可以忽略不计。

Conclusion: 该方案成功解决了集群AMT运行时中嵌套fork-join程序的SDC检测和纠正问题，并讨论了该方案对通过futures协作的任务的适应性。

Abstract: The trend of increasing cluster sizes of supercomputers leads to a growing susceptibility to Silent Data Corruption (SDC) that can invalidate program results. A common strategy for SDC protection is replication, where the computation is repeated, and the correct result is determined as the one that is the same in at least two different computations. Applying replication to Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) runtimes on clusters is challenging due to dynamic task spawning and work stealing, which complicate the identification of replicated tasks.
  To address the challenge, this paper introduces a novel replication scheme that detects and corrects SDCs for nested fork-join programs. Briefly stated, our approach replicates the computation and records the task tree. Upon a mismatch in the final result, it traverses the tree top-down to identify all corrupted tasks that could have impacted the final result. Recovery is then performed by recomputing these tasks, while the results of correct child tasks are reused.
  We demonstrate our implementation within a variant of the Itoyori cluster AMT runtime. Our experimental results suggest that the time to identify and reprocess the affected tasks is negligible. The paper concludes by discussing the adaptability of our scheme to tasks that cooperate through futures.

</details>


### [340] [Design and Implementation of a Serverless MapReduce Framework for Scalable Data Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29573)
*Angelos Dorotheos Chatzopoulos,Babis Andreou,Kakia Panagidi,Stathes Hadjiefthymiades*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种运行在Kubernetes/Knative上的事件驱动MapReduce框架，利用Apache Kafka和FaaS原则实现物流数据的实时处理，支持基于工作负载的可配置扩展和按需处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代物流系统从GPS、IoT传感器等来源持续产生大量数据，数据的聚合、处理和分析对于监控运营、优化效率和快速响应决策任务至关重要，需要高效的实时数据处理解决方案。

Method: 设计了一个事件驱动的MapReduce框架，运行在Kubernetes与Knative平台上，采用Apache Kafka作为组件间通信骨干。系统由五个松耦合服务组成，用于实时接收、处理和聚合数据；使用Redis保存工作流元数据，AWS S3提供持久化存储；结合MapReduce编程模型与FaaS原则，支持基于工作负载和底层硬件的可配置扩展。

Result: 实验评估表明，随着输入数据量的增加，系统能够有效扩展，同时支持scale-to-zero和按需处理能力。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了物流环境下的实时数据处理，具备良好的可扩展性和资源利用效率，为物流系统的数据驱动决策提供了有效支撑。

Abstract: Modern logistics systems tend to generate continuous streams of data from sources such as GPS, IoT sensors, and logistics management systems. The aggregation, processing, and analysis of data have become vital for monitoring operations, optimizing efficiency, and responding quickly to decision making tasks. In this paper, an event-driven MapReduce framework for real-time data processing in logistics environments is presented. This system runs on Kubernetes with Knative and utilizes Apache Kafka as the backbone for communication between the components. This platform is composed of five loosely coupled services that receive, process, and aggregate the incoming data in real-time. Redis is used to preserve workflow metadata, while an AWS S3 service provides persistent storage for the framework. The design is inspired by the MapReduce programming model. It integrates Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) principles with distributed processing techniques that allow configurable scaling based on the workload demands and the underlying hardware. Experimental evaluation shows that the system can scale effectively as the input data volume increases while supporting scale-to-zero, on-demand processing.

</details>


### [341] [TC-MIS: Maximal Independent Set on Tensor-cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29604)
*Prajjwal Nijhara,Dip Sankar Banerjee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了TC-MIS算法，首次利用GPU张量核心加速最大独立集(MIS)问题求解，通过将图遍历转化为稀疏矩阵向量乘法(SpMV)，在多种现代GPU架构上实现了相比现有方法最高44.38倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大独立集问题是图论中的基础问题，广泛应用于资源分配、调度和网络优化。现有GPU算法虽然已经取得良好性能，但图结构的非规则性导致内存访问不规则和工作负载不均衡，限制了并行效率。现代GPU配备了张量核心(TC)，其吞吐量是CUDA核心(CC)的8-16倍，已在机器学习领域广泛应用，但在图算法领域仍未被充分探索。因此，作者希望利用张量核心的高吞吐量特性来加速MIS问题的求解。

Method: TC-MIS算法的核心是将MIS计算的关键阶段重新表述为稀疏矩阵向量乘法(SpMV)。具体方法包括：(1)将图邻接矩阵进行分块处理，(2)采用Warp矩阵乘累加(WMMA)操作，(3)将不规则的图遍历转化为规则的、大规模并行计算。这种转换使得算法能够充分利用张量核心的高吞吐量特性。

Result: 在支持张量核心的多种微架构构(Ampere、Ada Lovelace、Hopper、Blackwell)上进行了评估。TC-MIS在RTX A5000上平均加速2.84倍，在L40S上加速4.84倍，在H200上加速18.80倍，在RTX 5080上加速5.20倍。其中在H200 GPU上达到最大加速比44.38倍。同时，算法保持了与现有启发式方法相当的解的质量，能够产生接近最大独立集的解。

Conclusion: TC-MIS成功证明了张量核心在图算法领域的应用潜力，通过将不规则图遍历转化为规则矩阵运算，实现了显著的性能提升。该工作为图算法在GPU上的优化提供了新思路，展示了专用硬件单元在非传统应用场景中的价值。

Abstract: Maximal Independent Set (MIS) in a graph is a fundamental problem with applications in resource allocation, scheduling, and network optimization. Although graphs are inherently un-structured and challenging for GPU parallelism due to irregular memory access and workload imbalance, specialized GPU algorithms have achieved good performance, processing million-vertex graphs in milliseconds. Modern GPUs are equipped with Tensor Cores (TCs), specialized units for matrix operations with 8-16x higher throughput than CUDA Cores (CCs), which are extensively used for ML, DL, and inference tasks but remain largely unexplored for graph algorithms. In this paper, we present TC-MIS, a TC-accelerated algorithm that reformulates key phases of MIS computation as sparse matrix-vector multiplication (SpMV). TC-MIS tiles the graph adjacency matrix and employs Warp Matrix Multiply-Accumulate (WMMA) operations to transform irregular graph traversal into regular, massively parallel computation. Our evaluation across TC-enabled microarchitectures (Ampere, Ada Lovelace, Hopper, Blackwell) demonstrates that TC-MIS achieves an average speedup of 2.84x on RTX A5000, 4.84x on L40S, 18.80x on H200 GPUs, and 5.20x on RTX 5080 with a maximum speedup of 44.38x on H200 GPU over state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining solution quality comparable to that obtained by established heuristics that produce near-maximum independent sets.

</details>


### [342] [AMDP: Asynchronous Multi-Directional Pipeline Parallelism for Large-Scale Models Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29664)
*Ling Chen,Houming Wu,Wenjie Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出AMDP方法，通过限制第一阶段处理批次数量、多管道并发和梯度累积，在保持高利用率的同时解决异步流水线并行中的参数不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异步流水线并行方法在前向和反向传播之间存在参数不匹配，导致收敛性能下降。

Method: AMDP采用三种策略：1) 限制每个流水线第一阶段在反向传播前最多处理两个小批次；2) 根据流水线深度启动多个并发流水线；3) 跨小批次累积梯度并在单次更新中应用。

Result: 在GPT和BERT风格模型上的实验表明，AMDP在保持收敛性能的同时显著加速了训练过程。

Conclusion: AMDP成功地在高利用率与收敛稳定性之间取得平衡，为大规模模型训练提供了有效的异步流水线并行解决方案。

Abstract: Pipeline parallelism is essential for large-scale model training, but existing asynchronous approaches often degrade convergence due to parameter mismatch between forward and backward passes. We propose Asynchronous Multi-Directional Pipeline parallelism (AMDP) to mitigate this issue while sustaining high utilization. AMDP limits the first stage of each pipeline to process at most two minibatches before backpropagation, bounding the number of parameter updates between forward and backward passes. To alleviate the resulting pipeline bubbles, AMDP launches multiple concurrent pipelines and adapts their number according to pipeline depth. In addition, AMDP accumulates gradients across minibatches and applies them in a single update, ensuring that only a bounded number of minibatches experience parameter mismatch, limited to within one optimization step. Experiments on GPT- and BERT-style models demonstrate that AMDP significantly accelerates training while preserving convergence.

</details>


### [343] [PRISM: Processing-In-Memory Sparse MTTKRP for Tensor Decomposition Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29728)
*Daniel Pacheco,Leonel Sousa,Aleksandar Ilic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM，首个利用存内计算(PIM)技术加速稀疏张量分解核心算子spMTTKRP的方法，相比CPU实现最高加速2.64倍，且资源利用效率显著优于CPU和GPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏张量分解广泛应用于机器学习，其中CP-ALS算法最耗时操作spMTTKRP具有强内存受限特性，难以在通用处理器上高效实现。存内计算技术为突破内存墙限制提供了新机遇。

Method: 提出PRISM框架，针对UPMEM PIM架构深入刻画不同分区策略、数据格式和内核优化，并设计CPU-PIM异构协作机制，将内存密集型操作卸载到PIM执行。

Result: PIM方案和异构方案相比最优CPU实现分别达到2.37倍和2.64倍加速；峰值性能利用率显著高于CPU和GPU；但在某些负载下UPMEM分布式内存系统会影响性能。

Conclusion: 存内计算技术能有效加速内存受限的稀疏张量分解操作，在性能和资源效率方面展现出优势，为稀疏张量计算提供了新思路。

Abstract: Sparse tensors are the most used representation of sparse multidimensional data. Operations that decompose them, selecting their most important features while reducing their dimension, have become prevalent procedures in machine learning. One of the most used tensor decomposition algorithms is the Alternating Least Squares Canonical Polyadic Decomposition (CP-ALS), where the most time-consuming operation is the Sparse Matricized Tensor Times Khatri-Rao Product (spMTTKRP). This operation is strongly memory-bound, making it hard to implement efficiently on general-purpose processors. This work proposes PRISM, the first approach to tackle this operation using Processing-In-Memory (PIM) technology. We extensively characterize different partitioning strategies, number formats, and kernel optimizations that efficiently adapt this operation to UPMEM PIM, which is further boosted by heterogeneous collaboration with the CPU. The experimental results show that the proposed PIM-based and heterogeneous approaches achieve up to 2.37x and 2.64x speedup compared to state-of-the-art CPU implementations, respectively. However, the UPMEM distributed memory system can significantly hinder performance on certain workloads. Nonetheless, the efficiency of resource consumption for this approach, measured by peak performance fraction usage, is significantly higher than for both CPU and GPU.

</details>


### [344] [CARM Tool: Cache-Aware Roofline Model Automatic Benchmarking and Application Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29740)
*José Morgado,Leonel Sousa,Aleksandar Ilic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨架构的自动化工具框架，支持x86（Intel/AMD）、ARM和RISC-V等主流CPU架构的Cache-aware Roofline Model（CARM）构建与应用分析，实验表明构建的CARM屋顶线误差小于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着HPC系统和CPU架构日益复杂，应用开发与优化极具挑战性。现有CARM工具覆盖范围有限，存在厂商特定（Intel Advisor）、开发不足（ARM）或完全缺失（AMD、RISC-V）的问题，迫切需要跨架构的自动化CARM支持工具。

Method: 开发针对现代CPU全性能谱（从标量到所有支持的向量ISA扩展）的汇编微基准测试，覆盖计算单元和所有存储层次；结合性能计数器和动态二进制插桩技术，在CARM框架内集成应用分析功能。

Result: 实验结果表明，通过该自动化框架构建的CARM屋顶线在各种测试架构峰值上的偏差小于1%，验证了方法的准确性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作成功填补了跨架构CARM工具支持的空白，为x86、ARM和RISC-V等主流架构提供了统一的自动化性能建模与分析能力，为应用开发与优化提供了有效指导。

Abstract: In recent years, HPC systems and CPU architectures as their central components, have become increasingly complex, making application development and optimization quite challenging. In this respect, intuitive performance models like the Cache-aware Roofline Model (CARM) offer effective guidance by providing insights into bottlenecks that limit the application's ability to reach the system's maximum performance. To fully exploit the benefits of CARM optimization guidance for application development, automatic tools for cross-architecture model construction and in-depth application characterization are absolutely essential. Given a plethora of existing CPU architectures, the current landscape of CARM-enabled tools covers either vendor-specific (Intel Advisor), not sufficiently developed (ARM) or simply non-existing (AMD, RISC-V) tools. This is a particular gap that this work intends to close by bringing automatic CARM support to all major CPU architectures and ISAs, i.e., x86 (Intel, AMD), ARM, and RISC-V, by developing assembly microbenchmarks specifically tailored to cover a full performance spectrum of modern CPUs (from scalar to all supported vector ISA extensions) for both computational units and all memory hierarchy levels. Additionally, this work integrates application analysis within the CARM framework using performance counters and dynamic binary instrumentation. Experimental results show that the CARM roofs constructed with the proposed automated framework provide less than a 1% deviation across various tested architectural maximums.

</details>


### [345] [Effective MPI: User-defined Datatypes and Cartesian Communicators for Zero-copy All-to-all Communication in Multidimensional Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29970)
*Jesper Larsson Träff*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对任意d维环面的零拷贝全通信算法，通过分解进程数并利用MPI派生数据类型实现高效实现，实验表明其性能可与原生MPI_Alltoall实现相媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高性能计算中，全通信操作是常见且关键的集合操作。对于任意维度的环面拓扑结构，如何高效实现全通信操作是一个重要问题。传统实现可能需要显式的本地数据重排序，开销较大。本文旨在设计一种针对d维环面的零拷贝全通信算法，以提高通信效率并减少内存开销。

Method: 该算法将进程数p分解为d个因子，映射到d维环面上。利用MPI的笛卡尔通信器将进程分成d个维度上的子通信器，并缓存这些通信器以避免重复分割的开销。全通信操作被分解为d个维度上的MPI_Alltoall操作的序列。关键在于使用MPI派生数据类型实现隐式的数据重排，避免了显式的本地数据重排序，从而实现了零拷贝。算法还采用了双缓冲方案，仅需适度的临时缓冲区。通过选择p的分解方式和对组件MPI_Alltoall操作的实现，可对算法进行调优以适应特定的高性能系统。

Result: 实验结果表明，该算法的性能可与原生MPI_Alltoall实现相媲美，甚至在某些情况下更具优势。同时，实验也揭示了常见MPI_Alltoall实现可能存在的问题。该算法提供了丰富的调优机会，可根据特定高性能系统进行适配。

Conclusion: 本文提出的针对任意d维环面的零拷贝全通信算法在MPI中有效实现，展示了与原生实现相媲美的性能，并通过实验验证了其有效性。该算法为高性能计算中的全通信操作提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We present and show how to implement a non-trivial all-to-all communication algorithm for arbitrary $d$-dimensional tori effectively in MPI. Given a factorization of the number of processes $p$ into $d$ factors that can be mapped onto a $d$-dimensional torus, we first utilize a Cartesian communicator to split a given $p$-process MPI communicator into, for each MPI process, $d$ smaller communicators spanning each of the dimensions of the torus to which the process belongs, and cache these communicators in order to avoid expensive splitting at each all-to-all operation. The all-to-all operation itself is decomposed into a sequence of $d$ MPI_Alltoall operations on the dimension-wise communicators. The non-trivial data rearrangement before and after each MPI_Alltoall call is implicit only and effected by MPI derived datatypes. This makes the implementation of the algorithm formally \emph{zero-copy}, meaning that no explicit process-local reordering of data blocks ever has to be performed. In order to achieve this, the algorithm employs a double-buffering scheme with modest temporary buffer requirements. By choosing the factorization of $p$ and selecting appropriate implementations for the component MPI_Alltoall operations, the presented implementation gives ample opportunities for algorithm tuning and adaptation to the particular high-performance system. A few, select experimental results show competitive performance with native MPI_Alltoall implementations and illustrate problems that common MPI_Alltoall implementations may have.

</details>


### [346] [RAFI -- A Ray/Work Forwarding Infrastructure for Data Parallel Multi-Node/Multi-GPU Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30294)
*Ingo Wald,Serkan Demirci,Alper Sahistan,Stefan Zellmann,Andrea Paris,Patrick Moran,Milan Jaros,Tatiana von Landesberger,Ugur Gudukbay,Valerio Pascucci*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了RaFI，一个基于CUDA和MPI的软件框架，用于简化构建GPU数据并行软件，特别是处理工作项在不同GPU间迁移的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多GPU环境下，当光线追踪或类似工作项需要在不同GPU之间迁移时，开发者面临复杂的CUDA和MPI编程挑战。现有方法要求开发者手动处理设备间通信、内存管理和同步等底层细节，这增加了开发难度和出错概率。

Method: RaFI提供了一个简洁的接口，允许CUDA内核直接将工作项转发到其他GPU。框架在底层自动管理所有CUDA和MPI相关的操作，包括设备间通信、内存传输、消息打包与解包、以及同步机制，对开发者透明。

Result: 通过多个示例应用验证了RaFI的有效性，展示了其在简化GPU间工作项迁移方面的能力，同时证明了该框架能够处理实际工作负载并提供良好的性能表现。

Conclusion: RaFI成功抽象了GPU间工作项迁移的复杂性，为开发者提供了一个易用的编程接口，降低了多GPU数据并行软件的开发门槛，为类似应用场景提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We present RaFI, a CUDA and MPI based software framework that simplifies the task of building GPU-enabled data-parallel software where rays or similar work items need to migrate between different GPUs. RaFI provides a simple interface for CUDA kernels to forward such work items to other GPUs, while under the hood managing all the CUDA and MPI related work required to make this happen. We describe RaFI's motivation and implementation, and show its potential in several example applications.

</details>
